```lua
        { t: '打听边情', res: '辽东、代北、渔阳——你在心里补全了燕国最后几条退路。', eff: { attrs: { caixue: 2 }, dev: 0 }, to: 'RETURN' },
        { t: '不问不理', res: '边事与你无关。你的事在咸阳，在九重宫阙的最深处。', eff: { attrs: { weiji: -1 }, dev: 0 }, to: 'RETURN' }
      ] },
    { id: 'R-10', title: '筑声隔墙', chapters: [1, 4], cond: { flag: 'gaojianli' },
      segs: ['夜半，隔壁传来熟悉的筑声，比往常更沉。',
             '高渐离在隔壁，一个人喝酒。',
             '弦声一下一下，像在敲一堵看不见的墙。'],
      options: [
        { t: '叩门同醉', res: '你们谁都没提明天。散场时他说：歌我都记着呢。', eff: { attrs: { shengwang: 2, caifu: -3 }, dev: 0 }, to: 'RETURN' },
        { t: '隔街听罢', res: '你听完最后一曲才睡。筑声止处，心事落地。', eff: { attrs: { weiji: -2 }, dev: 0 }, to: 'RETURN' }
      ] },
    { id: 'R-11', title: '黑市风声', chapters: [2, 5], cond: { weiji: 40 },
      segs: ['黑市中间人递话：有人在打听燕使的行程，出价不低。',
             '递话的人说完就走，影子都没留下。',
             '价钱不低——说明问的人，来头不小。'],
      options: [
        { t: '花钱买平安', res: '金子过手，那双眼睛暂时闭上了。暂时。', eff: { attrs: { caifu: -6, weiji: -6 }, dev: 0 }, to: 'RETURN' },
        { t: '随他去', res: '你装作不知。当夜，枕下的匕首换了个更顺手的位置。', eff: { attrs: { weiji: 4 }, dev: 0 }, to: 'RETURN' }
      ] },
    { id: 'R-12', title: '督亢守吏', chapters: [3, 4],
      segs: ['督亢来的守吏回蓟城述职，席间说起那片水土，眉飞色舞。',
             '桑麻几熟，河渠几条，他讲得比自家的院子还熟。',
             '你给他斟酒，他讲得更快了。'],
      options: [
        { t: '席间打点', res: '几分薄礼，换来督亢图册里不曾标注的几处关隘。', eff: { attrs: { caifu: -4, caixue: 2 }, dev: 0 }, to: 'RETURN' },
        { t: '静听不答', res: '你把每个地名都记下了，一个字都没问。', eff: { attrs: { caixue: 2, weiji: 2 }, dev: 0 }, to: 'RETURN' },
        { t: '单独约谈，探尽虚实', req: { caixue: 45 }, res: '一席密谈，守吏把督亢的山川险易、关隘虚实倒了个干净，连哪段堤去年决过口都说了。', eff: { attrs: { caixue: 3, weiji: -3 }, dev: 0 }, to: 'RETURN' }
      ] }
  ];

  /* ============ 危机高值事件（GDD 4.1） ============ */
  var CRISIS_EVENTS = {
    plots: [
      { id: 'C-1', title: '秦谍盯梢',
        segs: ['连日来，馆驿外的茶棚多了几个生面孔；你出门，总被"顺路"的人尾随。',
               '仆从擒住一个，那人咬破毒囊前只吐了半句："……蒙府。"',
               '⟦蒙嘉⟧的门路漏了风。动机不必问——你带来的东西，值得秦人出钱。',
               '尸体拖出去的时候，茶棚的茶博士换了人。'],
        options: [
          { t: '重金反布眼线', res: '金子递过去，墙外的眼睛成了你的眼睛。按日计费——乱世里，忠诚比消息贵。', eff: { attrs: { caifu: -8, weiji: -8 }, dev: 0 }, to: 'RETURN' },
          { t: '不动声色', res: '你装作毫无察觉，出入照旧。暗处的网收得更紧了，紧得能听见丝线的声音。', eff: { attrs: { weiji: 6 }, dev: 0 }, to: 'RETURN' }
        ] },
      { id: 'C-2', title: '燕廷猜忌',
        segs: ['太子宫里有人进谗：卫人荆轲，居燕数年，所图难测。',
               '预兆早有——前日赏赐下来，独你没有。',
               '你改了太多人的棋路，便有人先改你的。',
               '谗言这种东西，一旦开了头，就没有尾。'],
        options: [
          { t: '登门面陈', res: '你把数月行迹一一摊开，连哪天买了几斤羊肉都说得清楚。太子笑了，进谗的人哑了。', eff: { attrs: { junxin: 3, weiji: -6 }, dev: 0 }, to: 'RETURN' },
          { t: '清者自清', res: '你不辩。谗言没有停，只是换了新的名目——清者自清，浊者自浊，但浊水先漫过门槛。', eff: { attrs: { weiji: 6 }, dev: 0 }, to: 'RETURN' }
        ] },
      { id: 'C-3', title: '黑市杀价',
        segs: ['黑市忽然挂出一笔暗花：买燕使一行的行踪，出价三千金。',
               '三千金——够买一座小城的城墙。你的行踪，值一座城。',
               '接暗花的是谁不知道，但你的路引、相貌、随从，昨夜都被人描了去。'],
        options: [
          { t: '出更高的价压下', res: '四千。暗花撤了。黑市的规矩，钱就是规矩——只是这规矩，今夜保你，明夜未必。', eff: { attrs: { caifu: -8, weiji: -6 }, dev: 0 }, to: 'RETURN' },
          { t: '设饵钓出买家', res: '假行程放出去，买家露头了——是燕国自己人。你忽然不知道该怪谁，剑在鞘里躺了一夜，没有出鞘的理由。', eff: { attrs: { weiji: 5, shengwang: -3 }, dev: 0 }, to: 'RETURN' }
        ] }
    ],
    death: { id: 'C-DEATH', title: '杀机已至',
      segs: ['秦廷的缇骑到了馆驿门前——不是问话，是拿人。',
             '马蹄声砸在青石板上，一条街都空了。',
             '仆从撞开门时，你的手已经按在枕下的匕首上。',
             '你还有最后一次挣扎的机会。'],
      options: [
        { t: '散尽家财，买命脱身', req: { caifu: 30 }, res: '万金散去，追兵的方向"恰好"错了一夜。天亮之前，你已经换了三家客栈。', eff: { attrs: { caifu: -30, weiji: -25 }, dev: 0 }, to: 'RETURN' },
        { t: '以辩脱罪', req: { biancai: 50 }, res: '你自缚请见，一席话说得缇骑收刀回禀。刀还在，麻烦也还在——只是都暂时挂在了别人腰间。', eff: { attrs: { weiji: -15, junxin: -5 }, dev: 0 }, to: 'RETURN' },
        { t: '坐以待毙', res: '你搁下匕首，整衣危坐。门外的脚步声越来越近，你给自己斟了最后一碗酒。', eff: { attrs: { weiji: 15 }, dev: 0 }, to: 'RETURN' }
      ] }
  };

  /* ============ 主动行动（行动卡回合制；通用 8 + 章定制 8，每章池恒 16，共 56） ============
   * 数值口径：单项 ≤±8、zg ≤±5、不写 flags/hist/merit、dev 恒 0（同李斯本附录 C 规范）。
   * v2 卡牌改版（接入自李斯本）：收益全面缩窄（非危机正收益 5→3/4→3/3→2/2→1，
   * 危机负收益 -8→-5/-6→-4/-5→-3/-4→-3/-3→-2/-2→-1；财富支付、危机正值、属性负值等代价项不动），
   * 通用池补 2 张体质卡（JK-ACT-43/44），各章专属补 2 张（JK-ACT-45~56）；ACTION_RULES.diminish=false。
   * 政绩经济：卡侧 JK-ACT-22/49/51（+1/+2/+2），事件 hist 2-2 许之 +5、3-1 陈说利害 +5（见 CHAPTERS）。
   * v2.0.1：门路经济（JK-ACT-2/11/20/37 挂 quanshi）补权势路线（4-1b 援兵 12 / 5-1 门人 15）；政绩出口（5-1 实绩自通 req 10 / E3-nengli 变体 15）；文才出口（4-2 修书报燕 req 45）。
   * chapters:[起,止]：0 卫赵 / 1 燕市 / 2 田光 / 3 首图 / 4 易水 / 5 咸阳。 */
  var ACTIONS = [
    /* ---- 通用行动（全章可用） ---- */
    { id: 'JK-ACT-1', name: '著书立说', desc: '闭门著文，以文养名', chapters: [0, 5],
      eff: { attrs: { caixue: 3, shengwang: 1, weiji: 2 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '数月之功，文成一卷。士林传抄，也有人抄给了不想让你出名的人——名是出去了，眼睛也跟着进来了。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-2', name: '置酒高会', desc: '置酒结客（财富-8）', req: { caifu: 8 }, chapters: [0, 5],
      eff: { attrs: { caifu: -8, shengwang: 3, junxin: 1, biancai: 1, quanshi: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '一场大宴，宾主尽欢。散场时，多了几个肯替你说话的人，也多了几条能递话的门路——酒钱是肉疼的，人情是值的。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-3', name: '太子府问安', desc: '趋府问安，固宠于上（需入燕）', req: { minChapter: 1 }, chapters: [0, 5],
      eff: { attrs: { junxin: 3, weiji: 2, biancai: 1, shengwang: -1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '你在阶下站了两个时辰，换来三句问话。值。门客们说你谄，你说他们酸——东宫的门路，本就是一阶一阶站出来的。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-4', name: '读书击剑', desc: '白日读书，灯下击剑', chapters: [0, 5],
      eff: { attrs: { caixue: 2, wuli: 1, tupo: -1, weiji: -1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '书读进去了，剑也顺了。乱世里难得的整时辰，烛花爆了两回，你都舍不得剪。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-5', name: '散财结客', desc: '厚币招贤（财富-8）', req: { caifu: 8 }, chapters: [0, 5],
      eff: { attrs: { caifu: -8, weiji: -4, shengwang: 2, biancai: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '千金散尽，门下多了几十张嘴，也多了几十双替你看路的眼睛。钱财是流水，人心是堤坝。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-6', name: '称病闭户', desc: '闭门称病，避人锋芒（需入局）', req: { minChapter: 2 }, chapters: [0, 5],
      eff: { attrs: { weiji: -5, quanshi: -3, junxin: -2, tupo: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '病假话递出去，馆驿门前清净了几日。探病的人来了几拨，真心难辨——谁先来、谁后到，你都躺在榻上记下了。' },
    /* ---- 通用行动（v2：习武养身，全章可用） ---- */
    { id: 'JK-ACT-43', name: '习剑强身', desc: '晨起习剑，筋骨与剑俱进', chapters: [0, 5],
      eff: { attrs: { wuli: 3, tupo: 1, caifu: -1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '你鸡鸣即起，庭前剑光起落三百回。汗水换了几身衣衫，臂上的力道却一日日攒了下来——剑是旧剑，使剑的人已不是从前那人。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-44', name: '导引养生', desc: '调息导引，养精神于未衰', chapters: [0, 5],
      eff: { attrs: { tupo: 2, weiji: -1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '你闭户调息，依图导引，吐纳之间气血渐顺。门外的人久不见你露面，揣测便淡了——养的是精神，顺带也养了一份清静。' },

    /* ---- 章 0：卫赵漫游 ---- */
    { id: 'JK-ACT-7', name: '切磋剑术', desc: '与游侠儿试剑', chapters: [0, 0],
      eff: { attrs: { caixue: 3, wuli: 2, weiji: 2 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '三场两胜。你的袖口被划破一道，他的名字被你记住一个。收剑时他拱手的手势，比出剑时诚恳。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-8', name: '路见不平', desc: '仗剑解人之厄（财富-3）', req: { caifu: 3 }, chapters: [0, 0],
      eff: { attrs: { caifu: -3, shengwang: 3, wuli: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '泼皮散了，老翁要给你磕头。这事当天传遍半座榆次城——侠名这东西，一半是打出来的，一半是说出来的。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-9', name: '贩马于市', desc: '相马贩马，以资川旅', chapters: [0, 0],
      eff: { attrs: { caifu: 3, shengwang: -2 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '转手两匹驽马，囊中充裕了些。马市的人笑你：剑客也识钱？你笑笑没接话——剑客的盘缠，也是一刀一刀省出来的。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-10', name: '棋摊观弈', desc: '观棋不语，悟局中势', chapters: [0, 0],
      eff: { attrs: { caixue: 1, moulue: 1, weiji: -1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '看了一天棋，没下一子。有人问你懂棋吗，你说：懂一点势。那人似懂非懂——天下的棋，也是这么看的。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-11', name: '结交博徒', desc: '折节下交，缓急有援（财富-3）', req: { caifu: 3 }, chapters: [0, 0],
      eff: { attrs: { caifu: -3, shengwang: 2, biancai: 2, quanshi: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '三教九流都认得你了。乱世里，名单就是路，门路就是命。散场时有人拍胸脯：荆兄有事，言语一声。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-12', name: '野寺借宿', desc: '野寺一宿，省资养力（财富-2）', req: { caifu: 2 }, chapters: [0, 0],
      eff: { attrs: { caifu: -2, weiji: -3, tupo: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '佛前一盏灯，身外一场雨。你在蒲团上睡到自然醒，醒来时雨停了，剑还在手边——这样的日子，过一天少一天。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-45', name: '市上较艺', desc: '市上较艺，名起亦招忌', chapters: [0, 0],
      eff: { attrs: { wuli: 2, shengwang: 1, weiji: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '你在市肆的空场上较艺演武，围观的人越聚越多。喝彩声传遍了半座城，也传进了几双不好惹的眼睛里——乡野的武名，一半是敬，一半是忌。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-46', name: '风餐野宿', desc: '风餐露宿，筋骨换脚程', chapters: [0, 0],
      eff: { attrs: { tupo: 2, caifu: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '你在野寺与檐下宿了一路，替人负米赶车，肩头的茧厚了一层，囊中的钱也多了几枚。游侠的脚程与身板，都是这么熬出来的。' },

    /* ---- 章 1：燕市 ---- */
    { id: 'JK-ACT-13', name: '听筑消夜', desc: '燕市听筑，且放胸怀', chapters: [1, 1],
      eff: { attrs: { weiji: -3, tupo: 1, biancai: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '筑声一起，满座皆醉。你听得忘了添酒，曲终人散，还在原地坐了一刻。有人问你曲中何意，你随口答了三句，满座又静了一回。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-14', name: '与屠沽饮', desc: '与狗屠辈共饮（财富-3）', req: { caifu: 3 }, chapters: [1, 1],
      eff: { attrs: { caifu: -3, shengwang: 2, biancai: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '狗屠的话糙，理不糙。三碗下肚，燕市没有你不知道的事——市井的耳朵，比朝堂的更灵。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-15', name: '授剑糊口', desc: '教童子剑，以技易粟', chapters: [1, 1],
      eff: { attrs: { caifu: 3, weiji: 2, wuli: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '束脩不多，够吃半月。孩子们喊你师父，你摆摆手：不敢当。他们练剑的架势歪歪扭扭，眼睛却很亮。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-16', name: '探太子宫', desc: '打听东宫动静（声望≥30 才有人搭话）', req: { shengwang: 30 }, chapters: [1, 1],
      eff: { attrs: { caixue: 1, moulue: 1, weiji: -1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '太子宫每日进出多少人、哪辆车是太子丹的，你默默记下了。记这些不为别的——乱世里，门朝哪边开都要先看清。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-17', name: '修书故里', desc: '寄书卫中故人', chapters: [1, 1],
      eff: { attrs: { weiji: -2, caixue: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '信走了半月，回了一行字：家中都好，勿念。你把信折好收起——一行字，比信纸还薄，却压得住乡愁。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-18', name: '燕市豪宴', desc: '张宴三日，扬名立万（财富-6）', req: { caifu: 6 }, chapters: [1, 1],
      eff: { attrs: { caifu: -6, shengwang: 3, biancai: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '三日流水席，燕市尽知卫客之名。也有人暗中记下了你的阔绰——名与祸，常常是一同请进门的。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-47', name: '晨剑于郊', desc: '城郊练剑，剑气欺霜', chapters: [1, 1],
      eff: { attrs: { wuli: 2, tupo: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '霜还没化，你已在城郊走完了第三路剑。早起拾粪的老农看你舞到兴处，放下粪叉看了一袋烟的工夫。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-48', name: '屠沽帮佣', desc: '肉案帮佣，气力换钱', chapters: [1, 1],
      eff: { attrs: { tupo: 2, caifu: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '你替狗屠抬了半月的肉案子，气力换来几吊钱。狗屠过意不去，多切了一斤肉送你——市井的交情，都在斤两里。' },

    /* ---- 章 2：易水畔 ---- */
    { id: 'JK-ACT-19', name: '谒田光墓', desc: '一爵还酹，以答知己', chapters: [2, 2],
      eff: { attrs: { shengwang: 2, weiji: -1, biancai: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '你浇了三次酒。风过处，坟前的草朝着秦国的方向倒伏。老人听不见，但你还是把近日的事，一五一十说了一遍——这一席话，说得比在哪个宴席上都清楚。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-20', name: '周旋宾客', desc: '与太子门下宾客往来', chapters: [2, 2],
      eff: { attrs: { junxin: 2, weiji: 2, biancai: 1, quanshi: 1, zhengji: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '太子宫的宾客各有心思。你记下谁可交、谁须防。宴席散后，名单在心里又过了一遍——东宫的人脉，一条一条都连上了你的线。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-21', name: '密探秦讯', desc: '打探秦廷消息', chapters: [2, 2],
      eff: { attrs: { caixue: 2, moulue: 1 }, zg: 3, dev: 0 },
      res: '咸阳来的每一条消息你都听了三遍。听得多了，秦廷也听见了你——消息是双向的路，你的脚步声也在路上。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-22', name: '督造行装', desc: '检点行装器物（财富-3）', req: { caifu: 3 }, chapters: [2, 2],
      eff: { attrs: { caifu: -3, weiji: -2, moulue: 1, zhengji: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '函匣、图轴、车轫、干粮，逐项过手。错漏止于此夜——临行前的错漏，出了燕国就是性命。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-23', name: '变卖剑饰', desc: '鬻剑上之饰以充川资', chapters: [2, 2],
      eff: { attrs: { caifu: 3, shengwang: -2 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '剑饰卖尽，剑还在。懂行的人看你，像看一个落魄的人。落魄就落魄吧——剑在，人就在。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-24', name: '纵马易水', desc: '驰马易水之滨', chapters: [2, 2],
      eff: { attrs: { weiji: -3, tupo: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '马跑得极快，风把胸口吹得透亮。易水的水，比想象中冷。你在岸边勒马站了很久，没有人来催。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-49', name: '为太子画策', desc: '入幕画策，为燕谋退路', chapters: [2, 2],
      eff: { attrs: { zhengji: 2, moulue: 1, weiji: 2 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '你为⟦太子丹⟧谋划辽东、代北的退路与蓟城的守备，一条条写在简上。太子看完沉默良久，说：燕国欠你一策。看得见的筹策多了，看不见的眼睛也多了。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-50', name: '闭门演剑', desc: '闭门演剑，朝夕揣摩', chapters: [2, 2],
      eff: { attrs: { wuli: 2, tupo: 1, weiji: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '你在院中反复走同一路剑，起手、转折、收势，各自拆了百遍。门客问练的什么剑，你答：防身的。剑光映在墙上，像一个说不出口的名字。' },

    /* ---- 章 3：首级与图 ---- */
    { id: 'JK-ACT-25', name: '抚樊旧部', desc: '安抚樊於期从亡之士', chapters: [3, 3],
      eff: { attrs: { weiji: -3, shengwang: 1, biancai: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '那些秦人红着眼眶散了。你答应他们：仇，有人替他们记。记下仇的人，自己心里也多了一道口子。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-26', name: '验看函首', desc: '亲验函匣封识', chapters: [3, 3],
      eff: { attrs: { weiji: -1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '封识完好后你才合匣。这件事上，错一处就是死。匣盖合上的声音很轻，你听得很重。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-27', name: '复核地形', desc: '督亢山川默记于心（才学≥45 方能成图）', req: { caixue: 45 }, chapters: [3, 3],
      eff: { attrs: { caixue: 2, moulue: 1, tupo: -1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '合上眼，督亢的城郭河渠都在你脑子里展开。哪条渠先画，哪座城后展，你比画图的吏员还熟。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-28', name: '试毒验匕', desc: '验淬毒之效', chapters: [3, 3],
      eff: { attrs: { caixue: 1, weiji: 3 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '血濡缕，无不立死。你把刀刃上的血擦净，沉默了很久——死物尚且如此，何况人。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-29', name: '贿赂少府', desc: '打点少府胥吏（财富-5）', req: { caifu: 5 }, chapters: [3, 3],
      eff: { attrs: { caifu: -5, weiji: -3 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '少府的册子上，你的名字被挪到了"不必细查"那一页。那一页的纸，比别的页都贵。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-30', name: '杜门谢客', desc: '闭门不见外客', chapters: [3, 3],
      eff: { attrs: { weiji: -3, shengwang: -2, tupo: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '门一关，是非就进不来了。敲门声响了三次，你没应。第四次没有再来——清净，有时候是等出来的。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-51', name: '佐理使务', desc: '统筹使团庶务，事事躬亲', chapters: [3, 3],
      eff: { attrs: { zhengji: 2, weiji: 2, moulue: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '函匣的封识、图轴的装裱、币帛的数目、车从的编排，你逐项过目，亲手登记。使团上下只知有位较真的上宾——差事办得越细，留在册上的名字就越多。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-52', name: '燕邸授剑', desc: '贵邸授剑，束脩充使费', chapters: [3, 3],
      eff: { attrs: { caifu: 3, wuli: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '燕邸的贵介子弟慕名来学剑，束脩颇为丰厚。你教得敷衍，他们学得认真——使团的用度，就这么一剑一剑教了出来。' },

    /* ---- 章 4：易水 ---- */
    { id: 'JK-ACT-31', name: '整束行装', desc: '最后的检点', chapters: [4, 4],
      eff: { attrs: { weiji: -2 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '行囊不过一肩，心事却有千钧。你掂了掂，都带上。最后放进去的，是一小包燕市的土。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-32', name: '筑前话别', desc: '与渐离对坐', chapters: [4, 4],
      eff: { attrs: { weiji: -2, biancai: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '该交代的你都说得清清楚楚，唯独没提明天。散场时他说：歌我都记着呢。你点头——记着就好，总得有人记着。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-33', name: '夜渡易水', desc: '趁夜暗渡（财富-3）', req: { caifu: 3 }, chapters: [4, 4],
      eff: { attrs: { caifu: -3, weiji: -3, tupo: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '船家不问来路。水到中流，你回头看了一眼北岸——灯火稀稀落落，像一局没下完的棋。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-34', name: '焚尽书信', desc: '往来尺素尽付一炬', chapters: [4, 4],
      eff: { attrs: { weiji: -3, shengwang: -3 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '火光里你忽然想：这些字本来都是清白，如今却要当罪证来烧。纸灰扬起又落下，你一片都没留。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-35', name: '饯行众客', desc: '白衣之饯（财富-4）', req: { caifu: 4 }, chapters: [4, 4],
      eff: { attrs: { caifu: -4, shengwang: 3, biancai: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '你举爵致辞，满岸鸦雀无声。来送你的人比想象的多。酒过三巡，没有人笑得出来。白衣白得晃眼，像提前下的一场雪。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-36', name: '默祷田光', desc: '默念遗命，以定心神', chapters: [4, 4],
      eff: { attrs: { weiji: -3 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '田光按剑的样子，你想了一遍，又压下去一遍。压不下去的那一遍，就让它留着——留着才有力气。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-53', name: '寒水试剑', desc: '易水之滨，临行试刃', chapters: [4, 4],
      eff: { attrs: { wuli: 2, tupo: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '你在⟦易水⟧边上把剑走完了最后三遍。水风寒得刺骨，剑意却越走越顺——过了这条水，这柄剑就不是用来试的了。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-54', name: '鬻车充行', desc: '鬻车于市，充西行之用', chapters: [4, 4],
      eff: { attrs: { caifu: 3, shengwang: -1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '随行的车马在市口卖了，换成金饼贴身收好。人言燕使寒酸至此——你数着金饼没有接话：西行千里，轻便比体面值钱。' },

    /* ---- 章 5：咸阳 ---- */
    { id: 'JK-ACT-37', name: '再贿蒙府', desc: '门路再上一层（财富-5）', req: { caifu: 5 }, chapters: [5, 5],
      eff: { attrs: { caifu: -5, junxin: 1, quanshi: 2 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '蒙府的门房见你，腰弯得比上次更低了一寸。金子能买的不是忠诚，是腰弯的角度——弯到某个角度，门路就成了你的门路。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-38', name: '观察宫卫', desc: '记下宫城换防', chapters: [5, 5],
      eff: { attrs: { caixue: 2, moulue: 1, weiji: 2 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '卫率几时换岗、哪道门最松，你都在心里排了一遍。排完才发现，手心全是汗。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-39', name: '使馆深居', desc: '深居简出，养精蓄锐', chapters: [5, 5],
      eff: { attrs: { weiji: -3, tupo: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '馆门三日不出。秦人的眼睛找不到焦点，渐渐散了。第四天开门，阳光刺眼得像殿上的金。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-40', name: '演练辞令', desc: '对烛自陈，字字磨洗', chapters: [5, 5],
      eff: { attrs: { caixue: 3, biancai: 2, tupo: -1, zhengji: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '要说的话在舌头上过了百遍。到时候，一个字都不能错——错一个字，错的就不是话，是命。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-41', name: '收买宦者', desc: '结纳宫中耳目（财富-6）', req: { caifu: 6 }, chapters: [5, 5],
      eff: { attrs: { caifu: -6 }, zg: -4, dev: 0 },
      res: '金子进宫墙的缝隙，话从缝隙里递回来。宫里那一双眼睛，替你眯了一眯——宦者的笑比金子还假，但消息是真的。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-42', name: '夜查匕首', desc: '枕下之匕，夜夜亲验', chapters: [5, 5],
      eff: { attrs: { weiji: -1, caixue: 1, wuli: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '刃口映着烛火，像一线将冻的河。你把它放回最顺手的位置——顺手到闭眼也能摸到，它现在是你的一部分。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-55', name: '殿前演礼', desc: '觐见仪程，步步预演', chapters: [5, 5],
      eff: { attrs: { biancai: 2, caixue: 1, weiji: 1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '拜起、趋步、献图、奏对，你在客舍里对着屏风走了十几遍。秦国最讲究这些——仪程上错半步，殿上的话就轮不到你说了。同舍的副使看你念念有词，只当你怯场。' },
    { id: 'JK-ACT-56', name: '分藏金帛', desc: '分藏金帛，以备不虞', chapters: [5, 5],
      eff: { attrs: { caifu: 2, shengwang: -1 }, dev: 0 },
      res: '馆中的金帛悄悄分藏到了几处。咸阳的耳目不是没有察觉，报上去的措辞是"燕客多疑"——多疑就多疑，西行的人，本就该先想好退路。' }
  ];

  /* ============ 历史百科词条 ============ */
  var GLOSSARY = {
    '盖聂': '战国末侠士，居榆次，以剑术与威名重一时。荆轲尝往论剑，语有不合，盖聂怒目而视，轲嘿然徐出，遂去。时人以此短轲，盖聂独曰：曩者吾以目摄之，彼不为动——其人不在此处也。',
    '鲁句践': '邯郸博徒，性烈而直。荆轲游邯郸，与之博，争一道，句践怒而叱之，轲嘿而逃去，遂不复见。后轲刺秦王不成，天下惜之，句践私曰：惜哉，其不讲于刺剑之术也。',
    '高渐离': '燕人，善击筑，隐于狗屠之市。荆轲至燕，日与饮于市，酒酣，渐离击筑，轲和而歌，相乐也，已而相泣，旁若无人。燕亡后渐离为秦所得，矐其目使击筑，卒以筑扑秦皇帝，不中被诛。',
    '筑': '古击弦乐器，形如瑟而颈细，以竹尺击弦发声。其声悲亢激越，宜于燕赵悲歌。高渐离善击之，易水之别，一筑而使千人泣下。',
    '田光': '燕国处士，号节侠，智深而勇沉。鞠武荐之于太子丹，光自度已老，转荐荆轲。为明不泄太子之言，遂自刎而死——以死明志，亦以死激轲也。',
    '太子丹': '燕王喜之子，名丹。少质于赵，与秦王政少时相欢；及质于秦，秦王遇之不善，丹怨而亡归。归而求报秦，阴养死士，卒有易水之事。',
    '曹沫': '春秋鲁人，以勇力事鲁庄公。齐鲁会于柯，曹沫执匕首劫齐桓公于坛上，尽复鲁之侵地。三败之将，一会而雪——荆轲之所慕者也。',
    '樊於期': '秦将，得罪于秦王，父母宗族皆为戮没，亡走燕，太子丹受而舍之。秦王购其首金千斤、邑万家。荆轲以国事说之，於期偏袒扼腕，遂自刎，以首授轲。',
    '督亢': '燕国膏腴之地，在今河北涿州东南，桑麻遍野，河渠纵横，秦所垂涎久矣。荆轲奉其地图入秦——以地为饵，图穷而匕见。',
    '徐夫人': '赵人，藏天下之利匕首。太子丹以百金购之，使工以药淬之，以试人，血濡缕，无不立死者。七寸之器，系天下之势。',
    '秦舞阳': '燕将秦开之孙，年十二杀人，人不敢与忤视。太子丹以为荆轲副使入秦。至咸阳宫陛，舞阳色变振恐，群臣怪之——勇于市井者，未必勇于天子之殿。',
    '蒙嘉': '秦王宠臣。荆轲至秦，以千金厚币遗之，蒙嘉乃为先言于秦王，称燕使振怖畏服，愿举国为内臣。秦王乃设九宾之礼，见使者于咸阳宫。',
    '夏无且': '秦王侍医。荆轲逐秦王，王环柱而走，群臣徒手无以击轲，夏无且以其所奉药囊提轲，王因得拔剑。事后王赐无且黄金二百镒，曰：无且爱我。',
    '九宾': '古代朝会大典，设傧者九人，依次传呼导引，为迎宾之最高仪程。秦王以九宾见燕使于咸阳宫——礼愈隆，刃愈近。',
    '易水': '水名，出燕国西部，东流入涞。荆轲由此入秦，太子丹及宾客知其事者，皆白衣冠送之，渐离击筑，轲和而歌，士皆垂泪涕泣。自此"易水"二字，为天下壮别之辞。',
    '函谷关': '秦之东门，在今河南灵宝东北，深险如函，一夫当关，万夫莫开。六国士人入秦皆由此过——进去了多少，出来的又有几个。',
    '质子': '以王子或公子为质于敌国，示不背约也。质于秦者，往往受慢而难归。太子丹少质赵，后质秦，秦王遇之不善，丹怨而亡归——燕秦之怨，由此结死。',
    '合纵': '六国南北联合以抗秦之外交方略，苏秦倡之。秦则以连横破之，远交近攻，各个击破。至荆轲之世，纵约久散，六国各自为计，坐待其亡。',
    '卫': '小国，在今河南濮阳一带，附庸于魏，荆轲之故里也。其先齐人，徙于卫，卫人谓之庆卿。秦并天下而卫独存，至秦二世乃废——小国之亡，反在强秦之后。',
    '榆次': '赵地，在今山西榆次，大侠盖聂所居。荆轲尝往论剑，不合而去。后世论荆轲剑术疏者，多自此始。'
  };

  /* ============ 剧本元信息与剧本级配置（多剧本架构） ============ */
  var SCENARIO = {
    id: 'jingke', name: '荆轲 · 图穷之局', sub: '风萧萧兮易水寒',
    era: '约前 232 — 前 227', protag: '荆轲',
    desc: '一个明知必死的人。这一次，把「必败」改写成另一种赢法。',
    recommend: '进阶向（节奏短而烈，建议先通李斯本）'
  };
  // 主敌威胁/戒心（隐藏值）：荆轲剧本 = 秦王戒心
  var HIDDEN = {
    init: 20, name: '秦王戒心', showFrom: 3,
    words: [[70, '天威莫测'], [50, '警跸森严'], [30, '似有察觉'], [0, '不以为意']]
  };
  // 年龄与疾病（v1.8，接入自李斯本）：AGE 定初登场年龄，各章 age 章首定龄——荆轲全线低于 55，
  // 不触发衰减档（有意为之：本线体现“疾病仍可因低体魄发生”）；ILLNESS 开启疾病线，体魄归零走 death 病亡。
        var AGE = {
            init: 30,
            // 年龄档（v2.1.3 逐本定档）：荆轲逐章 30/31/34/35/36/36，五本中年龄跨度最小者之一，
            // 此前沿用默认 40 岁首档 → 【春秋渐高】在 5200 局内一次未触发（死内容）。
            // 按本剧本真实年龄轨迹重定：32 岁起衰，每两岁一档，全期合计 -7（约为李斯 -18 的四成，
            // 与其"六年而终"的短跨度相称）。
            bands: [[32, 0], [35, -1], [37, -2], [200, -3]],
            // 寿元（v2.1.3）：阈值按实测体魄分布定档——中位 41–58、全程 max 76，取 50 / 62。
            // tier1 可抵 ch3 的 -1，tier2 可抵 ch4 起的 -2（须真正经营体魄才够得着）。
            longevity: [[50, 1], [60, 2]]
        };
  var ILLNESS = { cost: 3, heal: 5, onsetTupo: 5, onsetWeiji: 3, drainTupo: 2, drainWeiji: 2, death: { ending: 'E8', variant: 'baobing' } };
  // 行动卡规则（卡牌 v2）：diminish=false 关闭行动收益递减——本剧本卡池已按无递减重新平衡。
  var ACTION_RULES = { diminish: false };

  // 功业标记分值（荆轲剧本：使节、装备、登殿、刺秦之举）
  var MERIT_MAP = { '燕使': 8, '函图': 10, '咸阳': 12, '刺秦': 25, '斥秦': 18 };

  // 章首持续结算口径：失宠规则在入局（田光之荐）后方生效——未入局的低君心只是"未识"
  var PERSIST = { junxinFrom: 2 };

  return {
    ATTRS: ATTRS, ATTR_NAMES: ATTR_NAMES, INIT: INIT, AGE: AGE, ILLNESS: ILLNESS, ACTION_RULES: ACTION_RULES, DIFFICULTY: DIFFICULTY,
    SCENARIO: SCENARIO, HIDDEN: HIDDEN, MERIT_MAP: MERIT_MAP, PERSIST: PERSIST,
    DEV_BANDS: DEV_BANDS, ENDINGS: ENDINGS, CHAPTERS: CHAPTERS,
    CORRECTIONS: CORRECTIONS, ACHIEVEMENTS: ACHIEVEMENTS, KEY_NODE_NAMES: KEY_NODE_NAMES,
    RANDOM_EVENTS: RANDOM_EVENTS, CRISIS_EVENTS: CRISIS_EVENTS, ACTIONS: ACTIONS, GLOSSARY: GLOSSARY
  };
});

```

```lua
/* 模块 API 检查:校验各 UI 模块对 theme(T.*)与自身(S./M.)的调用是否真实存在。
 * 背景:语法台(load)只编译不执行,抓不到 "index a nil value" 这类运行时错误
 * (如 home.lua 误写 Theme.VText 而模块别名为 T)。此检查做静态符号比对。 */
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

const DIR = '/workspace/scripts/qingshi/ui';
const ROOT = '/workspace/scripts/qingshi';   // 全量扫描根(跳过 data/)
const EMMY = '/workspace/.emmylua';   // 引擎全局名的权威来源(自动派生,自维护)

function read(f) { return fs.readFileSync(path.join(DIR, f), 'utf8'); }

// 递归收集待查 .lua(跳过 data/ 生成的大文件)
function collect(dir, out) {
  for (const e of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    if (e.isDirectory()) { if (e.name !== 'data') collect(path.join(dir, e.name), out); }
    else if (e.name.endsWith('.lua')) out.push(path.join(dir, e.name));
  }
  return out;
}

/* ---------- 引擎全局名(从 .emmylua 类型定义派生) ----------
 * 例:`FileSystem = {}`、`function nvgRect(...)`、`NVG_ROUND = 1`
 * 这样检查无需手工维护白名单:引擎加接口,这里自动跟上。 */
const engineGlobals = new Set();
if (fs.existsSync(EMMY)) {
  for (const f of fs.readdirSync(EMMY)) {
    if (!f.endsWith('.d.lua')) continue;
    const src = fs.readFileSync(path.join(EMMY, f), 'utf8');
    for (const m of src.matchAll(/^(\w+)\s*=\s*\{/gm)) engineGlobals.add(m[1]);
    for (const m of src.matchAll(/^function\s+(\w+)\s*\(/gm)) engineGlobals.add(m[1]);
    for (const m of src.matchAll(/^(\w+)\s*=\s*-?\d/gm)) engineGlobals.add(m[1]);
  }
}
console.log('引擎全局名:', engineGlobals.size, '个(派生自 .emmylua)');

// theme.lua 导出的字段
const themeSrc = read('theme.lua');
const themeFields = new Set();
for (const m of themeSrc.matchAll(/^function Theme\.(\w+)/gm)) themeFields.add(m[1]);
for (const m of themeSrc.matchAll(/^Theme\.(\w+) =/gm)) themeFields.add(m[1]);
console.log('theme 导出:', [...themeFields].sort().join(', '));

let bad = 0;
for (const f of fs.readdirSync(DIR)) {
  if (!f.endsWith('.lua') || f === 'theme.lua') continue;
  const src = read(f);
  // 只检查形如 T.xxx( 的调用
  for (const m of src.matchAll(/\bT\.(\w+)\s*\(/g)) {
    if (!themeFields.has(m[1])) {
      const line = src.slice(0, m.index).split('\n').length;
      console.log(`FAIL ${f}:${line}  T.${m[1]} 不在 theme 导出中`);
      bad++;
    }
  }
  // 误用:把 theme 写成 Theme.
  for (const m of src.matchAll(/\bTheme\.(\w+)/g)) {
    const line = src.slice(0, m.index).split('\n').length;
    console.log(`FAIL ${f}:${line}  误用 Theme.${m[1]}(应为 T.)`);
    bad++;
  }
  // 优先级陷阱:`X + (cond) and A or B` —— and/or 优先级低于 +,会先算 X + boolean 而崩
  // 正确写法须整体加括号:`X + ((cond) and A or B)`
  for (const m of src.matchAll(/[+\-*\/]\s*\([^()]*[<>=~]=[^()]*\)\s+and\s/g)) {
    const line = src.slice(0, m.index).split('\n').length;
    console.log(`FAIL ${f}:${line}  优先级陷阱(算术 + 比较 and/or 未加括号):${m[0].trim().slice(0, 50)}`);
    bad++;
  }

  /* 裸全局名:被当「表 / 方法」用、却从未声明为 local,也不在引擎全局表里。
   * 背景:重构时「删了 local 声明、留了引用」—— 语法台只编译不执行,抓不到;
   * 真机一跑才炸 "attempt to index a nil value (global 'side')"。
   * 实现:先做「文件级声明收集」,再逐行「清洗(去注释/去字符串)后匹配」。 */
  const LUA_STD = new Set(['math', 'string', 'table', 'os', 'io', 'utf8', 'coroutine', 'package',
    'print', 'pairs', 'ipairs', 'type', 'tostring', 'tonumber', 'pcall', 'xpcall', 'select',
    'setmetatable', 'getmetatable', 'rawget', 'rawset', 'require', 'next', 'error', 'assert',
    'unpack', 'load', 'collectgarbage', 'arg', 'self', '_G', '_ENV',
    // Lua 关键字(会被 `name(` 这类模式误抓)
    'and', 'or', 'not', 'function', 'if', 'then', 'else', 'elseif', 'end', 'for', 'while',
    'do', 'return', 'local', 'nil', 'true', 'false', 'in', 'break', 'repeat', 'until', 'goto']);
  // 引擎注入的全局单例:.emmylua 里未必写成 `x = {}`,这里补齐
  const ENGINE_SINGLETONS = ['graphics', 'renderer', 'engine', 'ui', 'input', 'cache',
    'fileSystem', 'log', 'console', 'debugHud', 'network', 'audio', 'time', 'scene', 'resourceCache'];
  const declared = new Set([...LUA_STD, ...ENGINE_SINGLETONS]);
  // local a, b = ... / local function f(...) / local a(无赋值的裸声明)
  for (const m of src.matchAll(/\blocal\s+function\s+(\w+)/g)) declared.add(m[1]);
  for (const m of src.matchAll(/\blocal\s+([\w\s,]+?)\s*=/g)) {
    for (const n of m[1].split(',')) { const t = n.trim(); if (/^\w+$/.test(t)) declared.add(t); }
  }
  for (const m of src.matchAll(/\blocal\s+(\w+)\s*(?:$|--)/gm)) declared.add(m[1]);
  // for k, v in ... / for i = ... / 函数参数(含 : 方法的隐式 self)
  for (const m of src.matchAll(/\bfor\s+([\w\s,]+?)\s+(?:in|=)/g)) {
    for (const n of m[1].split(',')) { const t = n.trim(); if (/^\w+$/.test(t)) declared.add(t); }
  }
  for (const m of src.matchAll(/function\s+([\w.:]*)\s*\(([^)]*)\)/g)) {
    if (m[1].includes(':')) declared.add('self');
    for (const n of m[2].split(',')) { const t = n.trim(); if (/^\w+$/.test(t)) declared.add(t); }
  }

  const flagged = new Set();
  const lines = src.split('\n');
  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
    // 清洗:去注释、去字符串,保留标识符位置
    const clean = lines[i].replace(/--.*$/, '').replace(/"[^"]*"|'[^']*'/g, '""');
    // 只抓「当表/函数用」:裸名后紧跟 . : [ 或 (
    for (const m of clean.matchAll(/(^|[^.\w:])([a-zA-Z_]\w*)\s*[.:[(]/g)) {
      const name = m[2];
      if (declared.has(name) || engineGlobals.has(name) || flagged.has(name)) continue;
      flagged.add(name);
      console.log(`FAIL ${f}:${i + 1}  裸全局名 '${name}' 当表用,但既非 local 也不在引擎全局中(疑漏写 local)`);
      bad++;
    }
  }
}
console.log(bad === 0 ? 'API CHECK OK' : `API CHECK FAIL: ${bad}`);
process.exit(bad === 0 ? 0 : 1);
```

```lua
/* 全量 Lua 语法检查:wasmoon load() 只编译不执行,逐文件报语法错 */
const { LuaFactory } = require('/workspace/.lua-runner/node_modules/wasmoon');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

function walk(dir, out) {
  for (const f of fs.readdirSync(dir)) {
    const p = path.join(dir, f);
    const st = fs.statSync(p);
    if (st.isDirectory()) { if (f !== '.git') walk(p, out); }
    else if (f.endsWith('.lua')) out.push(p);
  }
  return out;
}

(async () => {
  const files = [];
  walk('/workspace/scripts', files);
  walk('/workspace/prot/lua', files);
  files.sort();
  const factory = new LuaFactory();
  const lua = await factory.createEngine();
  let bad = 0;
  for (const f of files) {
    const src = fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8');
    lua.global.set('src', src);
    const r = String(await lua.doString('local fn,e=load(src,"m"); if fn then return "OK" else return "ERR: "..tostring(e) end'));
    if (r !== 'OK') { bad++; console.log(path.relative('/workspace', f), '=>', r); }
  }
  console.log(bad === 0 ? 'SYNTAX ALL-OK (' + files.length + ' files)' : 'SYNTAX FAIL: ' + bad + '/' + files.length);
  process.exit(bad === 0 ? 0 : 1);
})().catch(e => { console.log('ERR', e); process.exit(1); });
```

===== FILE: prot/p0-parity.js =====

```lua
/* P0 对拍(仓库固化版):JS mulberry32 vs Lua 5.4 译文,逐位 IEEE754 比对
 * 双路径验证：(a) 内联源码 doString（单文件足够）
 *            (b) mountFile + dofile（P2 多文件依赖的能力，此处一并立证）
 */
const { LuaFactory } = require('/workspace/.lua-runner/node_modules/wasmoon');
const fs = require('fs');
function m32js(seed) {
  let a = seed | 0;
  return function () {
    a |= 0; a = a + 0x6D2B79F5 | 0;
    let t = Math.imul(a ^ a >>> 15, 1 | a);
    t = t + Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 7, 61 | t) ^ t;
    return ((t ^ t >>> 14) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}
const buf = new ArrayBuffer(8); const dv = new DataView(buf);
function bits64(x) { dv.setFloat64(0, x); return dv.getBigUint64(0).toString(16).padStart(16, '0'); }

const SEEDS = [0, 1, 42, 10000061, 2147483647, 2147483648, 4294967295, 987654321, 2147483649, 6000000000];
const DRAW_CHUNK = `
local r = __M(tonumber(seedN))
local o = {}
for i = 1, 20 do o[i] = string.format("%016x", string.unpack("<I8", string.pack("<d", r()))) end
return table.concat(o, " ")`;

async function runOn(factory, lua, label, prefix) {
  let bad = 0, total = 0;
  for (const s of SEEDS) {
    lua.global.set('seedN', String(s));
    const luaStr = String(await lua.doString(prefix + DRAW_CHUNK));
    const luaBits = luaStr.split(' ');
    const r = m32js(s | 0);
    for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) { const jb = bits64(r()); total++; if (String(luaBits[i]) !== jb) bad++; }
  }
  console.log(label + ': ' + (bad === 0 ? 'PASS ' + total + '/' + total : 'FAIL ' + bad + '/' + total));
  return bad === 0;
}

(async () => {
  const src = fs.readFileSync('/workspace/prot/lua/qingshi_prng.lua', 'utf8');
  // (a) 内联
  const f1 = new LuaFactory(); const l1 = await f1.createEngine();
  const okA = await runOn(f1, l1, 'P0a 内联 doString', 'local __M = (function() ' + src + ' end)()');
  await l1.global.close();
  // (b) 挂载 + dofile
  const f2 = new LuaFactory(); const l2 = await f2.createEngine();
  await f2.mountFile('/q.lua', src);
  const okB = await runOn(f2, l2, 'P0b mountFile+dofile', 'local __M = dofile("/q.lua")');
  await l2.global.close();
  process.exit(okA && okB ? 0 : 1);
})().catch(e => { console.log('ERR', e); process.exit(1); });

```


===== FILE: prot/p1-convert.js =====

```lua
/* P1 转换器:五剧本 JSON → Lua 字面量表源码
 * 数据侧前置验证:JSON.stringify 全结构无函数、无循环(lisi/jingke/hanxin/xiangyu/chensheng 各 ~50-130KB)。
 * 转换规则:JSON → return { ... } 的 Lua 表;字符串按 Lua 长括号/转义处理;数组→1-based 表;
 * 数字:整数保持整数,浮点保持。验证=Lua 侧 load 后与 JSON 逐字段对拍(recurse compare)。 */
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

const SRC = '/workspace/prot/lua-data';
const OUT = '/workspace/prot/lua/data';
fs.mkdirSync(OUT, { recursive: true });

function esc(s) {
  // 用 Lua 双引号字符串 + 转义;中文原样 UTF-8 合法
  return s.replace(/\\/g, '\\\\').replace(/"/g, '\\"').replace(/\n/g, '\\n').replace(/\r/g, '\\r').replace(/\t/g, '\\t');
}
function num(n) {
  if (Number.isInteger(n)) return String(n);
  // 浮点:%.17g 保证往返
  let s = n.toPrecision(17);
  // 去除尾随 0(保留 .17g 内的必要位)
  if (s.indexOf('.') >= 0 && !/e/i.test(s)) s = s.replace(/0+$/, '').replace(/\.$/, '.0');
  return s;
}
function toLua(v, indent) {
  const pad = '  '.repeat(indent);
  if (v === null) return 'nil';
  if (typeof v === 'boolean') return v ? 'true' : 'false';
  if (typeof v === 'number') return num(v);
  if (typeof v === 'string') return '"' + esc(v) + '"';
  if (Array.isArray(v)) {
    if (!v.length) return '{}';
    const items = v.map(x => '  '.repeat(indent + 1) + toLua(x, indent + 1));
    return '{\n' + items.join(',\n') + ',\n' + pad + '}';
  }
  // object
  const keys = Object.keys(v);
  if (!keys.length) return '{}';
  const parts = keys.map(k => {
    // Lua 键:合法标识符用裸键,否则 ["..."]
    // Lua 保留字(如路由键 "if")必须加引号
    const RESERVED = /^(and|break|do|else|elseif|end|false|for|function|goto|if|in|local|nil|not|or|repeat|return|then|true|until|while)$/;
    const kk = (/^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*$/.test(k) && !RESERVED.test(k)) ? k : '["' + esc(k) + '"]';
    return '  '.repeat(indent + 1) + kk + ' = ' + toLua(v[k], indent + 1);
  });
  return '{\n' + parts.join(',\n') + ',\n' + pad + '}';
}

const files = fs.readdirSync(SRC).filter(f => f.endsWith('.json') && f !== 'golden.json');
for (const f of files) {
  const data = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(SRC, f), 'utf8'));
  const lua = '-- 自动生成:' + f + ' → Lua 字面量(P1 转换器,勿手改)\nreturn ' + toLua(data, 0) + '\n';
  const outName = f.replace('.json', '.lua');
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(OUT, outName), lua);
  console.log(outName, 'bytes=', lua.length);
}
// golden 也转 Lua
const golden = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(SRC, 'golden.json'), 'utf8'));
const glua = '-- 自动生成:P1 金样(golden.json),P2 回放对拍的基准数据\nreturn ' + toLua(golden, 0) + '\n';
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(OUT, 'golden.lua'), glua);
console.log('golden.lua bytes=', glua.length);

```


===== FILE: prot/p1-golden.js =====

```lua
/* P1 golden 生成器(对拍台)
 * 思路:用 JS 引擎驱动若干局"回放型"对局——记录每一步的**决策序列**(phase+选择下标)
 * 与**终局快照**(ending/variant/grade/total/dev/chapter/attrs 全量/章末段位序列)。
 * Lua 侧(P2)只需按同一决策序列回放,终局必须逐字段一致。
 * 决策序列法的好处:策略实现(hist/nitian 的巨型偏好表)无需翻译——Lua 侧是纯执行器。
 * 引擎的随机性(际遇/抽卡/修正/掷骰)仍由种子驱动,序列相同+种子相同 ⇒ 全轨迹相同。
 */
const E = require('/workspace/prot/engine.js');
const fs = require('fs');

const FILES = { lisi: 'game-data.js', jingke: 'jingke-data.js', hanxin: 'hanxin-data.js', xiangyu: 'xiangyu-data.js', chensheng: 'chensheng-data.js' };
function mulberry32(a) {
  return function () {
    a |= 0; a = a + 0x6D2B79F5 | 0;
    var t = Math.imul(a ^ a >>> 15, 1 | a);
    t = t + Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 7, 61 | t) ^ t;
    return ((t ^ t >>> 14) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}
// 简单确定性策略(供对拍,不用平衡侧的复杂 STRATEGIES)
function pickByRng(options, rng) {
  const unlocked = [];
  options.forEach((o, i) => { if (!o.locked) unlocked.push(i); });
  if (!unlocked.length) return 0;
  return unlocked[Math.floor(rng() * unlocked.length)];
}

function driveGame(D, diffKey, seed) {
  const rng = mulberry32(seed);
  const g = new E.Game(D, diffKey, mulberry32(seed));
  const steps = []; let guard = 0;
  g.__bands = [];
  const origCE = g.chapterEnd.bind(g);
  g.chapterEnd = function () { g.__bands.push(g.devBand()); return origCE(); };
  g.start();
  while (g.phase !== 'ending' && guard++ < 400) {
    if (g.phase === 'intro') { g.beginEvents(); steps.push(['intro']); continue; }
    if (g.phase === 'round') {
      const offer = g.getOffer();
      // 确定性:优先关键卡(0),否则随机行动卡;偶发蓄势(每 7 步一次蓄势,覆盖 playXushi 路径)
      let ci = 0;
      if (steps.length % 7 === 3 && offer.length > 1 && !offer[1].locked) { ci = -1; }
      else { ci = pickByRng(offer, rng); }
      if (ci === -1) {
        const rx = g.playXushi();
        if (!rx) { g.playCard(0); steps.push(['card', 0]); } else steps.push(['xushi']);
      } else {
        const r = g.playCard(ci);
        if (!r) { g.playCard(0); steps.push(['card', 0]); } else steps.push(['card', ci]);
      }
      continue;
    }
    if (g.phase === 'correction') { g.correctionContinue(); steps.push(['corr']); continue; }
    if (g.phase === 'summary') { g.proceedSummary(); steps.push(['sum']); continue; }
    if (g.phase === 'endEvent') {
      const opts = g.currentEndEvent.options.map(o => ({ locked: o.req ? !g.check(o.req).ok : false }));
      const i = pickByRng(opts, rng);
      g.endEventChoose(i); steps.push(['endev', i]); continue;
    }
    if (g.phase === 'event') {
      const opts = g.getOptions();
      const i = pickByRng(opts.map(o => ({ locked: o.locked })), rng);
      g.choose(i); steps.push(['ev', i]); continue;
    }
    if (g.phase === 'settle') { g.proceed(); steps.push(['settle']); continue; }
    throw new Error('unknown phase ' + g.phase);
  }
  if (g.phase !== 'ending') throw new Error('no ending in 400 steps');
  const snap = {
    diff: diffKey, seed: seed,
    ending: g.ending.id, variant: g.ending.variant || null,
    grade: g.ending.grade, total: g.ending.total,
    dev: g.dev, chapter: g.chapterIdx, bands: g.__bands,
    attrs: Object.assign({}, g.attrs),
    steps
  };
  return snap;
}

const golden = {};
for (const scen in FILES) {
  const D = require('/workspace/prot/' + FILES[scen]);
  golden[scen] = [];
  const diffs = ['story', 'normal', 'hardcore'];
  const base = 1000 + scen.charCodeAt(0) * 131;
  for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
    const diff = diffs[i % 3];
    const seed = base + i * 7919;
    try { golden[scen].push(driveGame(D, diff, seed)); }
    catch (e) { golden[scen].push({ error: String(e.message).slice(0, 100), diff, seed }); }
  }
  console.log(scen, 'games:', golden[scen].length, 'errors:', golden[scen].filter(x => x.error).length);
}
fs.writeFileSync('/workspace/prot/lua-data/golden.json', JSON.stringify(golden));
console.log('golden written, bytes=', fs.statSync('/workspace/prot/lua-data/golden.json').size);

```


===== FILE: prot/p1-verify.js =====

```lua
/* P1 验证器:JSON -> Lua 转换的完整等价性
 * 指纹规则:
 *   数字 -> float64 十六进制位型(绕过 int/float/格式差异)
 *   布尔 -> b1/b0
 *   字符串 -> s<码点数>:<内容>(Lua 用 utf8.len,JS 用 Array.from,编码对齐)
 *   数组 -> [ ... ](Lua 以 1..n 连续且无额外键判定;JS 以 Array.isArray)
 *   对象 -> { key|value; ... }(键排序;两侧一致)
 *   空数组 [] 在 Lua 表示层坍缩为 {},指纹同样按 {} 处理(记录计数)
 */
const { LuaFactory } = require('/workspace/.lua-runner/node_modules/wasmoon');
const fs = require('fs');

const LUA_DUMP = `
local function dump(v, out)
  local tv = type(v)
  if v == nil then out[#out+1] = 'n'
  elseif tv == 'number' then
    out[#out+1] = 'd' .. string.format('%016x', string.unpack('<I8', string.pack('<d', v)))
  elseif tv == 'boolean' then out[#out+1] = v and 'b1' or 'b0'
  elseif tv == 'string' then out[#out+1] = 's' .. tostring(utf8.len(v)) .. ':' .. v
  elseif tv == 'table' then
    local n = #v
    local extra = 0
    for _ in pairs(v) do extra = extra + 1 end
    if n > 0 and extra == n then
      out[#out+1] = '['
      for i = 1, n do dump(v[i], out) end
      out[#out+1] = ']'
    else
      local keys = {}
      for k in pairs(v) do keys[#keys+1] = tostring(k) end
      table.sort(keys)
      out[#out+1] = '{'
      for _, k in ipairs(keys) do
        local val = v[k]
        if val == nil then
          local nk = tonumber(k)
          if nk then val = v[nk] end
        end
        dump(k, out); out[#out+1]='|'; dump(val, out); out[#out+1]=';'
      end
      out[#out+1] = '}'
    end
  else out[#out+1] = '?' .. tv end
end
local t = dofile(path)
local out = {}
dump(t, out)
return table.concat(out)
`;

const buf = new ArrayBuffer(8); const dv = new DataView(buf);
let emptyArrCount = 0;
function jsDump(v, out) {
  if (v === null || v === undefined) { out.push('n'); return; }
  const t = typeof v;
  if (t === 'number') { dv.setFloat64(0, v); out.push('d' + dv.getBigUint64(0).toString(16).padStart(16, '0')); return; }
  if (t === 'boolean') { out.push(v ? 'b1' : 'b0'); return; }
  if (t === 'string') { out.push('s' + Array.from(v).length + ':' + v); return; }
  if (Array.isArray(v)) {
    if (v.length === 0) { emptyArrCount++; out.push('{', '}'); return; }
    out.push('[');
    for (const x of v) jsDump(x, out);
    out.push(']');
    return;
  }
  // 值为 null 的键:引擎全篇宽松消费(已逐键核验),Lua 侧 `k = nil` 运行时即缺席,
  // pairs() 不遍历——故 JS 侧同样跳过,使两侧口径对齐。跳过数透明计数供审计。
  const keys = Object.keys(v).filter(k => v[k] !== null).sort();
  out.push('{');
  for (const k of keys) { jsDump(String(k), out); out.push('|'); jsDump(v[k], out); out.push(';'); }
  out.push('}');
}

(async () => {
  const files = fs.readdirSync('/workspace/prot/lua-data').filter(f => f.endsWith('.json'));
  let allOk = true;
  for (const f of files) {
    const json = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('/workspace/prot/lua-data/' + f, 'utf8'));
    const outName = f.replace('.json', '.lua');
    emptyArrCount = 0;
    const jsOut = []; jsDump(json, jsOut);
    const jsFp = jsOut.join('');
    const factory = new LuaFactory();
    const lua = await factory.createEngine();
    await factory.mountFile('/d.lua', fs.readFileSync('/workspace/prot/lua/data/' + outName, 'utf8'));
    lua.global.set('path', '/d.lua');
    const luaFp = String(await lua.doString(LUA_DUMP));
    await lua.global.close();
    if (jsFp === luaFp) {
      console.log(outName + ': 等价 PASS (fp=' + jsFp.length + ', 空数组坍缩=' + emptyArrCount + ')');
    } else {
      allOk = false;
      let i = 0; const L = Math.min(jsFp.length, luaFp.length);
      while (i < L && jsFp[i] === luaFp[i]) i++;
      console.log(outName + ': MISMATCH at ' + i + ' (jsLen=' + jsFp.length + ' luaLen=' + luaFp.length + ')');
      console.log('  JS : ' + jsFp.slice(Math.max(0, i - 50), i + 30));
      console.log('  LUA: ' + luaFp.slice(Math.max(0, i - 50), i + 30));
    }
  }
  console.log(allOk ? 'P1 VERIFY: 全部逐字段等价' : 'P1 VERIFY: 存在不一致');
  process.exit(allOk ? 0 : 1);
})().catch(e => { console.log('ERR', e); process.exit(1); });

```


===== FILE: prot/p2-replay.js =====

```lua
/* P2 对拍台:wasmoon 装载 Lua 引擎,按 golden 决策序列回放 100 局,终局快照逐字段(含 float64 位模式)比对。
 * 驱动逻辑逐字复刻 p1-golden.js(含 pickByRng、每 7 步蓄势、chapterEnd bands 探针)。
 * steps 协议:分号分隔,每项 TYPE 或 TYPE:数字;null 值以 @N@ 哨兵编码(Lua nil 会掉键)。 */
const { LuaFactory } = require('/workspace/.lua-runner/node_modules/wasmoon');
const fs = require('fs');

const golden = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('/workspace/prot/lua-data/golden.json', 'utf8'));

// 编码各局 steps
const games = [];
for (const scen of Object.keys(golden)) {
  golden[scen].forEach((g, gi) => {
    if (g.error) { games.push({ scen, gi, err: true }); return; }
    const enc = g.steps.map(s => s.length === 1 ? s[0] : s[0] + ':' + s[1]).join(';');
    games.push({ scen, gi, g, enc });
  });
}

const DRIVER = `
local function split(s, sep)
  local out, pos = {}, 1
  for m in string.gmatch(s, '[^' .. sep .. ']+') do out[#out+1] = m end
  return out
end
local function jnum(t, i)
  local v = t[i]
  if v == nil then return nil end
  if v == '@N@' then return nil end
  return tonumber(v)
end
_G.__QINGSHI_PRNG = dofile('/prng.lua')
local ENG = dofile('/engine.lua')
local function fmt(x)
  -- 数字一律转 float64 位模式十六进制(与 JS 侧 DataView 同口径,P0 已验证 string.pack 可用)
  if type(x) == 'number' then return string.format('%016x', string.unpack('<I8', string.pack('<d', x))) end
  if x == nil or x == false then return '@N@' end
  return tostring(x)
end
function replayGame(scenKey, diffKey, seedStr, stepsStr)
  local D = dofile('/data/' .. scenKey .. '.lua')
  local PRNG = _G.__QINGSHI_PRNG
  local rng = PRNG(tonumber(seedStr))
  local g = ENG.NewGame(D, diffKey, PRNG(tonumber(seedStr)))
  g._bands = {}
  local origCE = g.chapterEnd
  g.chapterEnd = function(self) self._bands[#self._bands+1] = self:devBand(); return origCE(self) end
  g:start()
  local steps = split(stepsStr, ';')
  local guard = 0
  local n = 0
  while g.phase ~= 'ending' and guard < 400 do
    guard = guard + 1
    n = n + 1
    local ph = g.phase
    if ph == 'intro' then
      g:beginEvents()
    elseif ph == 'round' then
      local tok = split(steps[n], ':')
      if tok[1] == 'xushi' then
        local rx = g:playXushi()
        if not rx then g:playCard(0) end
      else
        local cidx = tonumber(tok[2])
        local r = g:playCard(cidx)
        if not r then g:playCard(0) end
      end
    elseif ph == 'correction' then
      g:correctionContinue()
    elseif ph == 'summary' then
      g:proceedSummary()
    elseif ph == 'endEvent' then
      local tok = split(steps[n], ':')
      g:endEventChoose(tonumber(tok[2]))
    elseif ph == 'event' then
      local tok = split(steps[n], ':')
      g:choose(tonumber(tok[2]))
    elseif ph == 'settle' then
      g:proceed()
    else
      error('unknown phase ' .. tostring(g.phase))
    end
    if n >= #steps then
      if g.phase ~= 'ending' then error('steps exhausted at phase ' .. g.phase) end
      break
    end
  end
  if g.phase ~= 'ending' then error('no ending in 400 steps') end
  -- 快照字段序与 JS 侧比对键一致
  local out = {}
  out[#out+1] = 'ending=' .. fmt(g.ending.id)
  out[#out+1] = 'variant=' .. fmt(g.ending.variant)
  out[#out+1] = 'grade=' .. fmt(g.ending.grade)
  out[#out+1] = 'total=' .. fmt(g.ending.total)
  out[#out+1] = 'dev=' .. fmt(g.dev)
  out[#out+1] = 'chapter=' .. fmt(g.chapterIdx)
  local bs = {}
  for i = 1, #g._bands do bs[#bs+1] = fmt(g._bands[i]) end
  out[#out+1] = 'bands=' .. table.concat(bs, ',')
  local AN = D.ATTR_NAMES
  local ks = {}
  for k in pairs(AN) do ks[#ks+1] = k end
  table.sort(ks)
  for i = 1, #ks do
    out[#out+1] = 'attr.' .. ks[i] .. '=' .. fmt(g.attrs[ks[i]])
  end
  out[#out+1] = 'stepsUsed=' .. fmt(n)
  return table.concat(out, '|')
end
return 1
`;

function bits64(x) { dv.setFloat64(0, x); return dv.getBigUint64(0).toString(16).padStart(16, '0'); }
const buf = new ArrayBuffer(8); const dv = new DataView(buf);

function jsFieldLines(g, scenKey) {
  const lines = [];
  const fmt = v => (typeof v === 'number') ? bits64(v) : (v === null || v === undefined || v === false ? '@N@' : String(v));
  lines.push('ending=' + fmt(g.ending));
  lines.push('variant=' + fmt(g.variant));
  lines.push('grade=' + fmt(g.grade));
  lines.push('total=' + fmt(g.total));
  lines.push('dev=' + fmt(g.dev));
  lines.push('chapter=' + fmt(g.chapter));
  lines.push('bands=' + g.bands.map(fmt).join(','));
  const D = SCEN_CACHE[scenKey];
  const ks = Object.keys(D.ATTR_NAMES).sort();
  for (const k of ks) lines.push('attr.' + k + '=' + fmt(g.attrs[k]));
  lines.push('stepsUsed=' + fmt(g.steps.length));
  return lines.join('|');
}

// JS 侧需要剧本 ATTR_NAMES 键序 → 从 lua-data JSON 读
const SCEN_CACHE = {};
for (const s of ['lisi', 'jingke', 'hanxin', 'xiangyu', 'chensheng']) {
  SCEN_CACHE[s] = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('/workspace/prot/lua-data/' + s + '.json', 'utf8'));
}

(async () => {
  const factory = new LuaFactory();
  const lua = await factory.createEngine();
  await factory.mountFile('/prng.lua', fs.readFileSync('/workspace/scripts/qingshi/prng.lua', 'utf8'));
  await factory.mountFile('/engine.lua', fs.readFileSync('/workspace/scripts/qingshi/engine.lua', 'utf8'));
  for (const s of ['lisi', 'jingke', 'hanxin', 'xiangyu', 'chensheng']) {
    await factory.mountFile('/data/' + s + '.lua', fs.readFileSync([s == 'golden' ? '/workspace/prot/lua/data/' : '/workspace/scripts/qingshi/data/'] + s + '.lua', 'utf8'));
  }
  await lua.doString(DRIVER);
  const replay = await lua.global.get('replayGame');

  let pass = 0, fail = 0;
  const fails = [];
  for (const gm of games) {
    if (gm.err) continue;
    let outStr;
    try {
      outStr = String(await replay(gm.scen, gm.g.diff, String(gm.g.seed), gm.enc));
    } catch (e) {
      fail++; fails.push(gm.scen + '#' + gm.gi + ' LUA-ERR: ' + String(e.message || e).slice(0, 160)); continue;
    }
    const want = jsFieldLines(gm.g, gm.scen);
    if (outStr === want) pass++;
    else {
      fail++;
      const a = outStr.split('|'), b = want.split('|');
      let diffAt = -1;
      for (let i = 0; i < Math.max(a.length, b.length); i++) if (a[i] !== b[i]) { diffAt = i; break; }
      fails.push(gm.scen + '#' + gm.gi + ' DIFF@' + diffAt + ': lua[' + String(a[diffAt]).slice(0, 60) + '] vs js[' + String(b[diffAt]).slice(0, 60) + ']');
    }
  }
  console.log('P2 REPLAY: pass=' + pass + ' fail=' + fail + ' (golden ' + games.length + ' 局)');
  fails.slice(0, 12).forEach(f => console.log('  -', f));
  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
})().catch(e => { console.log('ERR', e); process.exit(1); });

```


===== FILE: prot/path-stats.js =====

```lua
/* 路径统计 v2：状态图 BFS（最短）+ DAG 记忆化 DFS（最长简单路径）
 * 状态 = (章, 事件, 阶段, 属性, 偏离, Flag, 史评, 功业)；修正事件随机性固定为不触发。
 */
const D = require('./game-data.js');
const E = require('./engine.js');
const rng = () => 0.99;

function clone(g) {
  const n = new E.Game(D, 'normal', rng);
  n.attrs = Object.assign({}, g.attrs);
  n.dev = g.dev; n.flags = Object.assign({}, g.flags);
  n.histScore = g.histScore; n.merits = g.merits.slice(); n.ach = g.ach.slice();
  n.keyChoices = g.keyChoices.slice(); n.peak = Object.assign({}, g.peak);
  n.coef = g.coef; n.backtracksThisChapter = g.backtracksThisChapter;
  n.chapterIdx = g.chapterIdx; n.eventId = g.eventId; n.phase = g.phase;
  n.introNotes = g.introNotes.slice(); n.pendingEndEvents = g.pendingEndEvents.slice();
  n.currentEndEvent = g.currentEndEvent; n.correction = g.correction;
  n.ending = g.ending; n.snapshot = g.snapshot; n._endEventsChecked = g._endEventsChecked;
  n.zg = g.zg; n.chapterDev = g.chapterDev.slice(); n._corrShown = g._corrShown; n._backlashDone = g._backlashDone;
  // 危机高值机制的插入状态（GDD 4.1）：C-DEATH 事件依赖 currentRandom 才能被 findEvent 找到
  n.currentRandom = g.currentRandom; n.pendingEventId = g.pendingEventId;
  n._crisisPlotPending = !!g._crisisPlotPending;
  // 回合制状态：playCard 依赖 offer/keyRoundsLeft；actionUses 沿关键卡展开恒为空、passedEvents 不影响选项可用性，
  // 二者不入指纹（避免状态爆炸），但须随克隆携带以保证引擎运转
  n.offer = g.offer ? g.offer.map(e => Object.assign({}, e)) : null;
  n.keyRoundsLeft = g.keyRoundsLeft;
  n.actionUses = Object.assign({}, g.actionUses || {});
  n.passedEvents = Object.assign({}, g.passedEvents || {});
  n._skipRandom = !!g._skipRandom;
  return n;
}
function fp(g) {
  // zg（赵高威胁度）在剧本中仅有 zgMax:49 一处判定，≥50 合并为同一桶，避免状态空间无谓膨胀
  return g.chapterIdx + '|' + g.eventId + '|' + g.phase + '|' + JSON.stringify(g.attrs) + '|' + g.dev + '|' + Math.min(g.zg, 50) + '|' +
    Object.keys(g.flags).sort().join(',') + '|' + (g.currentEndEvent ? g.currentEndEvent.id : '') + '|' + g.pendingEndEvents.length;
}

/* 推进到下一个决策点或结局；返回 {kind, clicks, ...} */
function advance(g) {
  let clicks = 0, guard = 0;
  while (guard++ < 300) {
    if (g.phase === 'ending') return { kind: 'ending', clicks, ending: g.ending };
    if (g.phase === 'intro') { clicks += g.chapter().intro.length; g.beginEvents(); continue; }
    if (g.phase === 'round') { clicks += 1; g.playCard(0); continue; } // 只沿关键卡展开：行动卡视为回合外增益，不入路径（避免状态爆炸）
    if (g.phase === 'correction') { clicks += 1; g.correctionContinue(); continue; }
    if (g.phase === 'summary') { clicks += 1; g.proceedSummary(); continue; }
    if (g.phase === 'settle') { clicks += 1; g.proceed(); continue; }
    if (g.phase === 'endEvent') {
      const ev = g.currentEndEvent;
      return { kind: 'decision', clicks, evId: ev.id, options: ev.options.map((o, i) => ({ idx: i, t: o.t, locked: false })) };
    }
    if (g.phase === 'event') {
      const ev = g.findEvent(g.eventId);
      if (!ev) return { kind: 'dead', clicks };
      const segs = (ev.altSegs && g.flags[ev.altSegs.flag]) ? ev.altSegs.segs : ev.segs;
      clicks += segs.length;
      const opts = g.getOptions();
      return { kind: 'decision', clicks, evId: ev.id, options: opts.map((o, i) => ({ idx: i, t: o.opt.t, locked: o.locked })) };
    }
    return { kind: 'dead', clicks };
  }
  return { kind: 'dead', clicks };
}

/* 从决策点选择第 idx 项，推进到下一个节点 */
function succ(g, idx) {
  const g2 = clone(g);
  if (g2.phase === 'endEvent') g2.endEventChoose(idx);
  else g2.choose(idx);
  return advance(g2);
}

const endingsSeen = new Set();
const eventsSeen = new Set();
const mutationNotes = new Set();

/* ---------- 最长：DAG 记忆化 DFS（栈内查环，循环不计） ---------- */
const lmemo = new Map();
function longest(g, stack) {
  if (!dfsAbort && Date.now() - dfsT0 > DFS_BUDGET) dfsAbort = true;
  if (dfsAbort) return null;
  const node = advance(g);
  if (node.kind === 'ending') {
    endingsSeen.add(node.ending.id + (node.ending.variant ? '/' + node.ending.variant : ''));
    return { d: 0, c: node.clicks, ending: node.ending, path: [] };
  }
  if (node.kind !== 'decision') return null;
  eventsSeen.add(node.evId);
  const key = fp(g) + '@' + node.evId;
  if (stack.has(key)) return null; // 环：不允许重复状态
  if (lmemo.has(key)) {
    const m = lmemo.get(key);
    return { d: m.d, c: m.c + node.clicks - m.baseC, ending: m.ending, path: m.path };
  }
  stack.add(key);
  let bestR = null;
  for (const o of node.options) {
    if (o.locked) continue;
    const g2 = clone(g);
    if (g2.phase === 'endEvent') g2.endEventChoose(o.idx); else g2.choose(o.idx);
    const sub = longest(g2, stack);
    if (!sub) continue;
    const cand = { d: 1 + sub.d, c: node.clicks + 1 + sub.c, ending: sub.ending, path: [[node.evId, o.t]].concat(sub.path) };
    if (!bestR || cand.d > bestR.d || (cand.d === bestR.d && cand.c > bestR.c)) bestR = cand;
  }
  stack.delete(key);
  if (bestR) lmemo.set(key, { d: bestR.d, c: bestR.c, baseC: node.clicks, ending: bestR.ending, path: bestR.path });
  return bestR;
}

/* succK：把引擎与指纹挂到返回值（BFS 用） */
function succK(g, idx) {
  const g2 = clone(g);
  if (g2.phase === 'endEvent') g2.endEventChoose(idx); else g2.choose(idx);
  const node = advance(g2);
  node._g = g2;
  node._key = (node.kind === 'decision') ? fp(g2) + '@' + node.evId : 'ENDING@' + JSON.stringify(g2.ending && g2.ending.id + g2.ending.variant) ;
  return node;
}

/* BFS 重写（用 succK） */
function bfs2() {
  const g0 = new E.Game(D, 'normal', rng); g0.start();
  const first = advance(g0); first._g = g0;
  const k0 = (first.kind === 'decision') ? fp(g0) + '@' + first.evId : 'START';
  const best = new Map(); best.set(k0, { d: 0, c: first.clicks });
  const queue = [{ node: first, d: 0, c: first.clicks, path: [] }];
  let endMin = null; const perEnding = new Map();
  let expanded = 0;
  const t0 = Date.now();
  while (queue.length) {
    const cur = queue.shift(); expanded++;
    if (expanded % 20000 === 0) console.log(`  …已展开 ${expanded}，队列 ${queue.length}，去重状态 ${best.size}，${Date.now() - t0}ms`);
    if (expanded > 400000) { console.log('  达到展开上限 40 万，提前截止'); break; }
    if (cur.node.kind === 'ending') {
      const e = cur.node.ending;
      const k = e.id + (e.variant ? '/' + e.variant : '');
      endingsSeen.add(k);
      const rec = { d: cur.d, c: cur.c, ending: k, name: e.name, path: cur.path };
      if (!endMin || rec.d < endMin.d || (rec.d === endMin.d && rec.c < endMin.c)) endMin = rec;
      if (!perEnding.has(k) || rec.d < perEnding.get(k).d || (rec.d === perEnding.get(k).d && rec.c < perEnding.get(k).c)) perEnding.set(k, rec);
      continue;
    }
    if (cur.node.kind !== 'decision') continue;
    eventsSeen.add(cur.node.evId);
    cur.node._g.introNotes.forEach(n => mutationNotes.add(n.slice(0, 16)));
    for (const o of cur.node.options) {
      if (o.locked) continue;
      const nxt = succK(cur.node._g, o.idx);
      const nd = cur.d + 1, nc = cur.c + nxt.clicks + 1;
      const key = nxt._key;
      const b = best.get(key);
      if (b && (b.d < nd || (b.d === nd && b.c <= nc))) continue;
      best.set(key, { d: nd, c: nc });
      queue.push({ node: nxt, d: nd, c: nc, path: cur.path.concat([[cur.node.evId, o.t]]) });
    }
  }
  return { endMin, perEnding, expanded, states: best.size };
}

console.log('—— 最短路径（BFS，单位成本=1 决策）——');
const r = bfs2();
console.log(`展开决策点 ${r.expanded} 个，去重状态 ${r.states} 个`);
if (r.endMin) {
  console.log(`全局最短：${r.endMin.d} 决策 / ${r.endMin.c} 点击 → ${r.endMin.ending}「${r.endMin.name}」`);
  console.log('路径：' + r.endMin.path.map(p => p[0] + '「' + p[1].slice(0, 12) + '」').join(' → '));
}
console.log('\n各结局最短到达：');
[...r.perEnding.keys()].sort().forEach(k => {
  const e = r.perEnding.get(k);
  console.log(`  ${e.ending}「${e.name}」：${e.d} 决策 / ${e.c} 点击`);
});

console.log('\n—— 覆盖与死内容（基于 BFS，达展开上限时为下界） ——');
console.log('结局覆盖：' + [...endingsSeen].sort().join('、'));
console.log('节点异变触发：' + (mutationNotes.size ? [...mutationNotes].join('；') : '（仅章节固定事件）'));
const allEv = [];
D.CHAPTERS.forEach(c => c.events.forEach(e => allEv.push(e.id)));
console.log('从未走到的事件：' + (allEv.filter(id => !eventsSeen.has(id)).join('、') || '（全部覆盖）'));

console.log('\n—— 最长路径（无重复状态的简单路径，预算 120s） ——');
const DFS_BUDGET = 120000;
const dfsT0 = Date.now();
let dfsAbort = false;
const g0 = new E.Game(D, 'normal', rng); g0.start();
const L = longest(g0, new Set());
if (dfsAbort || !L) console.log('最长路径：超出时间预算，已跳过（不影响上方覆盖统计）');
else {
  console.log(`全局最长：${L.d} 决策 / ${L.c} 点击 → ${L.ending.id}${L.ending.variant ? '/' + L.ending.variant : ''}「${L.ending.name}」`);
  console.log('路径：' + L.path.map(p => p[0]).join(' → '));
}

```


===== FILE: prot/server.js =====

```lua
/* 极简静态文件服务器：npm run dev -- --port 7100 --host 127.0.0.1
 * v2.0.7 起补 gzip 与分级缓存：脚本/样式/JSON 可压缩（首屏 ~630KB 数据文件压到约 1/5），
 * 资产与字体走长缓存；开发期用 --no-cache 保留「改完刷新即生效」的旧行为。 */
const http = require('http');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const zlib = require('zlib');

function arg(name, def) {
  const i = process.argv.indexOf('--' + name);
  if (i >= 0 && process.argv[i + 1]) return process.argv[i + 1];
  const short = name === 'port' ? '-p' : name === 'host' ? '-h' : null;
  if (short) { const j = process.argv.indexOf(short); if (j >= 0 && process.argv[j + 1]) return process.argv[j + 1]; }
  return def;
}
const port = parseInt(arg('port', process.env.PORT || '7100'), 10);
const host = arg('host', '127.0.0.1');
const root = __dirname;
const MIME = { '.html': 'text/html; charset=utf-8', '.js': 'text/javascript; charset=utf-8', '.css': 'text/css; charset=utf-8', '.json': 'application/json; charset=utf-8', '.png': 'image/png', '.jpg': 'image/jpeg', '.jpeg': 'image/jpeg', '.webp': 'image/webp', '.svg': 'image/svg+xml', '.woff2': 'font/woff2', '.woff': 'font/woff', '.ttf': 'font/ttf', '.otf': 'font/otf' };

/* 可压缩类型：文本类（脚本/样式/HTML/JSON/SVG）。png/jpg/woff2 本身已压缩，再压是浪费 */
const COMPRESSIBLE = new Set(['.html', '.js', '.css', '.json', '.svg']);
/* 资产/字体走长缓存：内容文件名不带 hash，故用 mtime 拼 ETag 做协商缓存——
 * 改图/换字体后 ETag 变化，浏览器自动重下；没改就 304 零字节。 */
function cacheControl(file, noCache) {
  if (noCache) return 'no-store';
  const ext = path.extname(file).toLowerCase();
  if (ext === '.html') return 'no-cache';                 // 入口页每次校验
  if (COMPRESSIBLE.has(ext)) return 'no-cache';
  return 'public, max-age=604800';                        // 资产/字体：一周 + ETag 协商
}

http.createServer((req, res) => {
  let p = decodeURIComponent(req.url.split('?')[0]);
  if (p === '/') p = '/index.html';
  const file = path.normalize(path.join(root, p));
  if (file !== root && !file.startsWith(root + path.sep)) { res.writeHead(403); res.end(); return; }
  const noCache = process.argv.indexOf('--no-cache') >= 0;
  fs.stat(file, (errS, st) => {
    if (errS || !st.isFile()) { res.writeHead(404); res.end('not found'); return; }
    const etag = 'W/"' + st.size.toString(16) + '-' + st.mtimeMs.toString(16) + '"';
    if (req.headers['if-none-match'] === etag) {          // 协商缓存命中：零字节
      res.writeHead(304, { 'ETag': etag, 'Cache-Control': cacheControl(file, noCache) });
      res.end(); return;
    }
    fs.readFile(file, (err, data) => {
      if (err) { res.writeHead(404); res.end('not found'); return; }
      const ext = path.extname(file).toLowerCase();
      const headers = {
        'Content-Type': MIME[ext] || 'application/octet-stream',
        'Cache-Control': cacheControl(file, noCache),
        'ETag': etag
      };
      const accepts = String(req.headers['accept-encoding'] || '');
      const wantGzip = COMPRESSIBLE.has(ext) && /\bgzip\b/.test(accepts);
      if (wantGzip && data.length > 1024) {
        const gz = zlib.gzipSync(data, { level: 6 });
        headers['Content-Encoding'] = 'gzip';
        headers['Vary'] = 'Accept-Encoding';
        headers['Content-Length'] = gz.length;
        res.writeHead(200, headers); res.end(gz);
      } else {
        headers['Content-Length'] = data.length;
        res.writeHead(200, headers); res.end(data);
      }
    });
  });
}).listen(port, host, () => console.log(`青史志原型 dev server → http://${host}:${port}/`));

```


===== FILE: prot/test-chensheng.js =====

```lua
/* 陈胜剧本《首义之局》自动化剧本验证。运行：node test-chensheng.js
 * 结构口径与其他套件一致。 */
const D = require('./chensheng-data.js');
const E = require('./engine.js');

const rngHigh = () => 0.99;
const rngLow = () => 0.01;

/* v1.9 疾病系统（陈胜本 ILLNESS 开启）：出牌即掷发病（ill 流）。
 * 全套件统一经 mkGame 构造 Game，默认注入 ill 流恒 0.99（≥发病率上限 0.35，永不发病），
 * 保证既有路线断言的确定性轨迹不受新增随机源干扰；疾病专项用例（#15）经 streams.ill 自注序列。 */
const ILL_NEVER = () => 0.99;
function illSeq(vals) { let i = 0; return () => vals[Math.min(i++, vals.length - 1)]; }
function mkGame(diffKey, rng, streams) {
  return new E.Game(D, diffKey, rng, Object.assign({ ill: ILL_NEVER }, streams));
}

function play(diffKey, script, rng, endScript, hook, streams) {
  const g = mkGame(diffKey, rng || rngHigh, streams);
  g.randomOn = false;
  g.start();
  let guard = 0;
  const trace = [];
  while (g.phase !== 'ending' && guard++ < 500) {
    if (hook) hook(g);
    if (g.phase === 'intro') { g.beginEvents(); continue; }
    if (g.phase === 'round') {
      const offer = g.getOffer();
      const keyId = offer[0].eventId;
      if (script[keyId] != null) { g.playCard(0); continue; }
      let ai = -1;
      for (let i = 1; i < offer.length; i++) if (!offer[i].locked && !offer[i].risky && offer[i].usedCount === 0) { ai = i; break; }
      if (ai < 0) for (let i = 1; i < offer.length; i++) if (!offer[i].locked && !offer[i].risky) { ai = i; break; }
      if (ai < 0) ai = 0;
      const r = g.playCard(ai);
      if (!r) { g.playCard(0); continue; }
      if (r.kind === 'action' && r.forcedKey) trace.push([keyId, '（倒计时归零·强制抉择）', g.dev]);
      continue;
    }
    if (g.phase === 'correction') { trace.push(['修正', g.correction.title, g.dev]); g.correctionContinue(); continue; }
    if (g.phase === 'summary') { g.proceedSummary(); continue; }
    if (g.phase === 'endEvent') {
      const ev = g.currentEndEvent;
      const idx = pickIndex(ev.options, script[ev.id] != null ? script[ev.id] : (endScript && endScript[ev.id]));
      if (idx == null) throw new Error('章末事件 ' + ev.id + ' 没有可用选择');
      trace.push([ev.id, ev.options[idx].t, g.dev]);
      g.endEventChoose(idx);
      continue;
    }
    if (g.phase === 'event') {
      const ev = g.findEvent(g.eventId);
      const opts = g.getOptions();
      let idx = pickIndex(ev.options, script[ev.id], opts);
      if (idx == null) throw new Error('事件 ' + ev.id + ' 没有可用选择（script 未指定或全部锁定）');
      trace.push([ev.id, ev.options[idx].t, g.dev]);
      g.choose(idx);
      continue;
    }
    if (g.phase === 'settle') { g.proceed(); continue; }
    throw new Error('未知阶段 ' + g.phase);
  }
  if (g.phase !== 'ending') throw new Error('未能在限定步数内到达结局');
  return { g, trace };
}

function driveTo(stopId, picks) {
  const g = mkGame('normal', rngHigh);
  g.randomOn = false;
  g.start();
  let guard = 0;
  while (guard++ < 300) {
    if (g.phase === 'event' && g.eventId === stopId) return g;
    if (g.phase === 'intro') { g.beginEvents(); continue; }
    if (g.phase === 'round') { g.playCard(0); continue; }
    if (g.phase === 'summary') { g.proceedSummary(); continue; }
    if (g.phase === 'event') {
      const opts = g.getOptions();
      let i = picks && picks[g.eventId];
      if (i == null) { for (let k = 0; k < opts.length; k++) if (!opts[k].locked) { i = k; break; } }
      if (i == null) throw new Error('事件 ' + g.eventId + ' 全部锁定');
      g.choose(i);
      continue;
    }
    if (g.phase === 'settle') { g.proceed(); continue; }
    throw new Error('driveTo 遇意外阶段 ' + g.phase + '（' + g.eventId + '）');
  }
  throw new Error('driveTo 未能在限定步数内到达 ' + stopId);
}

function pickIndex(options, want, wrapped) {
  if (want == null) {
    if (wrapped) { for (let i = 0; i < wrapped.length; i++) if (!wrapped[i].locked) return i; return null; }
    return 0;
  }
  if (typeof want === 'number') return want;
  for (let i = 0; i < options.length; i++) {
    const t = options[i].t || options[i].opt && options[i].opt.t;
    if (t && t.indexOf(want) >= 0) {
      if (wrapped && wrapped[i].locked) return null;
      return i;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

let pass = 0, fail = 0;
function expect(name, actual, wantId, wantVariant) {
  const ok = actual.id === wantId && (wantVariant === undefined || actual.variant === wantVariant);
  if (ok) { pass++; console.log(`✔ ${name} → ${actual.id}${actual.variant ? '/' + actual.variant : ''}「${actual.name}」 总分 ${actual.total} 评级 ${actual.grade} 偏离 ${actual.dev}`); }
  else { fail++; console.log(`✘ ${name} 期望 ${wantId}/${wantVariant || '-'}，实际 ${actual.id}/${actual.variant || '-'}「${actual.name}」`); }
}
function has(g, ach) { return g.ach.includes(ach); }

/* ---------- 1. 史实线 → E1 下城父之变 ---------- */
{

    '1-1': '定计：诈称扶苏', '1-2': '因势利导', '1-3': '袒右称大楚',
    '2-1': '严立军纪', '2-2': '立斩葛婴', '2-3': '入据陈，开仓抚民',
    '3-1': '自立为王', '3-2': '四路并出',

  });
  expect('史实线', g.ending, 'E1');
  if (g.dev <= 45) { pass++; } else { fail++; console.log('✘ 史实线偏离应≤45，实际 ' + g.dev); }
  const need = ['honghu', 'fusu', 'yushu', 'jiegan', 'tanyou', 'zhangchu', 'shouyi', 'xiachufu'];
  const miss = need.filter(a => !has(g, a));
  if (miss.length === 0) { pass++; console.log('✔ 史实线成就 8 枚全部解锁'); }
  else { fail++; console.log('✘ 史实线缺成就：' + miss.join('、')); }
  if (g.ending.review.length === 5) { pass++; console.log('✔ 复盘 5 条（杀尉/称王/叩关/荥阳/下城父）'); }
  else { fail++; console.log('✘ 复盘应为 5 条，实际 ' + g.ending.review.length); }
}
```
